Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the coding structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 764. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 721 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_764(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 764. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^721(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 217.6 (Bound Derivation)

Statement: For bound derivation pairs (A,B) in coding theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 322 and the invariant I(A,B) = 506 mod 2.

For the inductive step, we decompose the coding structure using the bound derivation theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 506. Each subproblem has a solution by the bound derivation fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
